Registrar abuse report to HOSTINGER operations, UAB

File at: abuse-tracker@hostinger.com
Subject: Phishing / brand impersonation: uniswapv4.com impersonates Uniswap

DRAFT - NOT FILED. File only after the brand owner approves it and has signed a Letter of Authorisation.

Hello HOSTINGER operations, UAB abuse team,

The domain uniswapv4[.]com (registered with you on 2026-10-05) is being used to impersonate Uniswap, a crypto project whose official site is hxxps://uniswap[.]org, https://uniswapfoundation[.]org, https://unichain[.]org. We ask you to suspend the domain under your abuse policy and the RAA section 3.18 obligations.

Evidence (observed 2026-11-01, read-only, URLs defanged):
- URL: hxxps://uniswapv4[.]com/
- Brand name plus a phishing lure word
- TLS certificate issued 2026-10-05 (Certificate Transparency)
- Brand named on the page (8x), including the title
- Wallet-connect prompt/library: Connect Wallet
- Resolves to 179.198.211.231
- Page title: "Uniswap V4"
- Screenshot: shots/uniswapv4.com.jpg (attach)

We are not affiliated with the operator of uniswapv4[.]com. Uniswap has not authorised its use of the brand. Screenshots and HTML captures are available on request.

Regards,
{{REPORTER_NAME}}
{{REPORTER_ORG}}, acting for Uniswap under a Letter of Authorisation dated {{LOA_DATE}}
{{REPORTER_EMAIL}}

4. Coverage and limits of this scan

  • Certificate Transparency (crt.sh): uniswap% 2169 certs; uniswap.% 2169 certs; uniswap-% 0 certs; %uniswap% 0 certs. crt.sh truncates leading-wildcard searches, so names that only contain the brand in the middle can be missed.
  • Newly registered domains (WhoisDS free list, capped at 70k names/day): 2026-10-31: 70000, 2026-10-30: 70000, 2026-10-29: 70000, 2026-10-28: 70000, 2026-10-27: 70000, 2026-10-26: 70000, 2026-10-25: 70000.
  • Tokens: base: 30 days of new pools; ethereum: 1.4 days of new pools (3 RPC errors). Only tokens that got a Uniswap/Aerodrome pool are seen. Free Ethereum RPCs serve about a day of logs.
  • Wallet blocklists checked: metamask, scamsniffer.
  • Not covered yet: X/Twitter, Telegram and Discord impersonators, app stores, paid ads, Solana tokens.
  • Scores are heuristics. Every finding above "low" should be looked at by a person before anything is filed.

5. How this was collected

Only public data was read: DNS, certificate logs, public domain lists, public blockchain logs, RDAP, and the public pages themselves (fetched like a normal visitor; no logins, no clicks, no form input, no wallet connected). No suspect was contacted. Nothing in this report has been filed with any registrar, host or blocklist: every report under "Draft" is text for Uniswap to approve. We file only with a signed Letter of Authorisation.
